[2025-08-31 02:00:14] ERROR - ❌ Failed during Gold Aggregates / Dashboard / Sheets step: (psycopg2.errors.DatatypeMismatch) UNION types integer and text cannot be matched
LINE 129:         SELECT *
                         ^

[SQL: 
    DROP TABLE IF EXISTS gold.dashboard CASCADE;
    CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS gold;

    CREATE TABLE gold.dashboard AS

    -- Aggregate per user
    WITH user_agg AS (
        SELECT 
            r.user_id,
            MIN(r.ride_date) FILTER (WHERE r.ride_status='completed') AS first_ride_date,
            MAX(r.ride_date) FILTER (WHERE r.ride_status='completed') AS last_ride_date
        FROM silver.rides r
        GROUP BY r.user_id
    ),

    -- Deduplicate feedback per ride_id, keep only first feedback
    feedback_dedup AS (
        SELECT *
        FROM (
            SELECT *,
                   ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ride_id ORDER BY feedback_id ASC) AS rn
            FROM silver.feedback
        ) t
        WHERE rn = 1
    ),

    -- Deduplicate payments per ride_id, keep only first payment
    payments_dedup AS (
        SELECT *
        FROM (
            SELECT *,
                   ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ride_id ORDER BY payment_id ASC) AS rn
            FROM silver.payments
        ) t
        WHERE rn = 1
    ),

               ua.last_ride_date,

               r.drop_loc,

               p.fare,
               p.discount_percent,
               p.discount_amount,
               p.final_amount,
               p.payment_status,
               f.feedback_id,
               f.user_rating,
               f.captain_rating AS feedback_captain_rating,
               f.issue_category,
               f.comments
        FROM silver.users u
        LEFT JOIN user_agg ua ON u.user_id = ua.user_id
        LEFT JOIN silver.rides r ON u.user_id = r.user_id
        LEFT JOIN silver.captains c ON r.captain_id = c.captain_id
        LEFT JOIN payments_dedup p ON r.ride_id = p.ride_id
        LEFT JOIN feedback_dedup f ON r.ride_id = f.ride_id
    ),

    -- Final dashboard, ensure one row per ride_id

[SQL: 
   DROP TABLE IF EXISTS gold.dashboard CASCADE;
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS gold;

CREATE TABLE gold.dashboard AS

-- Aggregate per user
WITH user_agg AS (
    SELECT 
        r.user_id,
        MIN(r.ride_date) FILTER (WHERE r.ride_status='completed') AS first_ride_date,
        MAX(r.ride_date) FILTER (WHERE r.ride_status='completed') AS last_ride_date
    FROM silver.rides r
    GROUP BY r.user_id
),

-- Deduplicate feedback per ride_id, keep only first feedback
feedback_dedup AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM (
        SELECT *,
               ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ride_id ORDER BY feedback_id ASC) AS rn
        FROM silver.feedback
    ) t
    WHERE rn = 1
),

-- Deduplicate payments per ride_id, keep only first payment
payments_dedup AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM (
        SELECT *,
               ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ride_id ORDER BY payment_id ASC) AS rn
        FROM silver.payments
    ) t
    WHERE rn = 1
),

-- Base join of users, rides, captains, feedback, payments
dashboard_base AS (
    SELECT 
        u.user_id,                              -- INTEGER
        u.name           AS user_name,         -- TEXT
        u.gender,                              -- TEXT
        u.age            AS user_age,          -- INTEGER
        u.signup_date,                         -- DATE
        u.city           AS user_city,         -- TEXT
        ua.first_ride_date,                    -- DATE
        ua.last_ride_date,                     -- DATE
        c.captain_id,                          -- INTEGER
        c.name           AS captain_name,      -- TEXT
        c.age            AS captain_age,       -- INTEGER
        c.city           AS captain_city,      -- TEXT
        c.rating         AS captain_rating_overall, -- NUMERIC (or FLOAT/DECIMAL)
        r.ride_id,                            -- INTEGER
        r.ride_date,                          -- TIMESTAMP
        r.pickup_loc,                         -- TEXT
        r.drop_loc,                          -- TEXT
        r.distance_km,                       -- NUMERIC
        r.duration_min,                      -- NUMERIC
        r.ride_status,                      -- TEXT
        p.payment_id,                       -- INTEGER
        p.payment_method,                   -- TEXT
        p.fare,                            -- NUMERIC
        p.discount_percent,                 -- NUMERIC
        p.discount_amount,                  -- NUMERIC
        p.final_amount,                    -- NUMERIC
        p.payment_status,                  -- TEXT
        f.feedback_id,                     -- INTEGER
        f.user_rating,                    -- NUMERIC
        f.captain_rating AS feedback_captain_rating, -- NUMERIC
        f.issue_category,                 -- TEXT
        f.comments                       -- TEXT
    FROM silver.users u
    LEFT JOIN user_agg ua ON u.user_id = ua.user_id
    LEFT JOIN silver.rides r ON u.user_id = r.user_id
    LEFT JOIN silver.captains c ON r.captain_id = c.captain_id
    LEFT JOIN payments_dedup p ON r.ride_id = p.ride_id
    LEFT JOIN feedback_dedup f ON r.ride_id = f.ride_id
),

-- Captains with no rides (explicit casts to match types exactly)
captains_no_rides AS (
    SELECT 
        NULL::INTEGER     AS user_id,
        NULL::TEXT        AS user_name,
        NULL::TEXT        AS gender,
        NULL::INTEGER     AS user_age,
        NULL::DATE        AS signup_date,
        NULL::TEXT        AS user_city,
        NULL::DATE        AS first_ride_date,
        NULL::DATE        AS last_ride_date,
        c.captain_id,                          -- INTEGER
        c.name            AS captain_name,    -- TEXT
        c.age             AS captain_age,     -- INTEGER
        c.city            AS captain_city,    -- TEXT
        c.rating          AS captain_rating_overall, -- NUMERIC
        NULL::INTEGER     AS ride_id,
        NULL::TIMESTAMP   AS ride_date,
        NULL::TEXT        AS pickup_loc,
        NULL::TEXT        AS drop_loc,
        NULL::NUMERIC     AS distance_km,
        NULL::NUMERIC     AS duration_min,
        NULL::TEXT        AS ride_status,
        NULL::INTEGER     AS payment_id,
        NULL::TEXT        AS payment_method,
        NULL::NUMERIC     AS fare,
        NULL::NUMERIC     AS discount_percent,
        NULL::NUMERIC     AS discount_amount,
        NULL::NUMERIC     AS final_amount,
        NULL::TEXT        AS payment_status,
        NULL::INTEGER     AS feedback_id,
        NULL::NUMERIC     AS user_rating,
        NULL::NUMERIC     AS feedback_captain_rating,
        NULL::TEXT        AS issue_category,
        NULL::TEXT        AS comments
    FROM silver.captains c
    WHERE NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 
        FROM silver.rides r 
        WHERE r.captain_id = c.captain_id
    )
)

-- Final dashboard, ensure one row per ride_id
SELECT *
FROM dashboard_base
UNION ALL
SELECT *
FROM captains_no_rides;

    ]

    DROP TABLE IF EXISTS gold.dashboard CASCADE;
    CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS gold;

    CREATE TABLE gold.dashboard AS

    -- Aggregate per user
    WITH user_agg AS (
        SELECT 
            r.user_id,
            MIN(r.ride_date) FILTER (WHERE r.ride_status='completed') AS first_ride_date,
            MAX(r.ride_date) FILTER (WHERE r.ride_status='completed') AS last_ride_date
        FROM silver.rides r
        GROUP BY r.user_id
    ),

    -- Deduplicate feedback per ride_id, keep only first feedback
    feedback_dedup AS (
        SELECT *
        FROM (
            SELECT *,
                   ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ride_id ORDER BY feedback_id ASC) AS rn
            FROM silver.feedback
        ) t
        WHERE rn = 1
    ),

    -- Deduplicate payments per ride_id, keep only first payment
    payments_dedup AS (
        SELECT *
        FROM (
            SELECT *,
                   ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ride_id ORDER BY payment_id ASC) AS rn
            FROM silver.payments
        ) t
        WHERE rn = 1
    ),

    -- Base join of users, rides, captains, feedback, payments
    dashboard_base AS (
        SELECT 
            u.user_id::TEXT,
            u.name::TEXT           AS user_name,
            u.gender::TEXT,
            u.age::INTEGER         AS user_age,
            u.signup_date::DATE,
            u.city::TEXT           AS user_city,
            ua.first_ride_date::DATE,

            p.payment_status::TEXT,
            f.feedback_id::TEXT,
            f.user_rating::NUMERIC,
            f.captain_rating::NUMERIC AS feedback_captain_rating,
            f.issue_category::TEXT,
            f.comments::TEXT
        FROM silver.users u
